```
def calibrate_orient():
    n=8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
    x=rng.rand(n); target=n/2; x=project_feasible(x,n)

    w=w/w.sum()
    # numerical gradient central eps=1e-6
    eps=1e-6
    grad_num=zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
       xp=x.copy(); xp[i]=min(1,max(0,xp[i]+eps)); xm... (if at bound? choose interior x [0.1,0.9] to avoid)
       cP=...; cM=...
       grad_num[i]=(float(w@ (cP-cM)) -2? no w dot c difference )/(2eps)
    # analytic candidates using weighted_gradient on a=1-2x
    a=1-2x

    g2=...
    # choose with smaller error relative to norm, if ties orient=0
```

`weighted_gradient` can be top-level. Let's implement efficiently:
```
def weighted_gradient(w, a, n, orient):
    # w shape 2n-1
    L=2*n-1
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if orient == 0: # variable i gets a[i+k], k=q-(n-1)
       # loop q
       for q, wq in enumerate(w):
          if wq ==0: continue
          k=q-(n-1)
          if k>=0:
             if n-k>0: g[:n-k] += wq * a[k:]
          else:
             if n+k>0: g[-k:] += wq * a[:n+k]
    else: # variable i gets a[i-k]
       for q,wq...
          if k>=0: g[k:] += wq * a[:n-k]
          else: g[:n+k] += wq * a[-k:]
    return g
```
This loops L up to 800, each slice length average n/2 ->160k operations, good. For gradient called many times (L-BFGS 1000s) =>160M per 1000? Wait 160k per gradient, 1000 =>160M, okay. But Python loop 800 per gradient *1000 =>800k iterations, fine. Use local variables for speed. Could optimize by skipping zero weights; at high alpha weights concentrated few peaks, so faster.

Pseudo best_improve:
```
def best_swap_improve(h,c,n,orient,cross_sign,max_sweeps,deadline):
  a=1-2h
  M=int(c.max())
  ones=list(np.where(h==1)[0]); zeros=list(np.where(h==0)[0])
  for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
     improved=False
     best_delta=None; best_i=None; best_j=None; best_newM=M; best_improvement=0
     # Option: only consider candidates that affect peak lags to reduce; but can just all.
     for i in zeros:
       # maybe precompute slice start/end
       for j in ones:
          delta=zeros(L)
          add slices i and -j
          delta[n_1]-=2
          delta[cross_idx_ij]+=1; delta[cross_idx_ji]+=1
          nc=(c+delta).max()
          if nc < M: # any improvement
             # choose best (lowest nc) or first? Best requires compare all.
             if nc < best_newM or (nc==best_newM and ?):
                store
     if no best: break
     apply
  return
```

Implement `project_box_sum(v, n, target)`:
```
def project_box_sum(v, n, target):
    # v float
    # find lambda such that sum(clip(v-lambda,0,1)) = target
    lo = max(v) - target/n? Actually if lambda high, sum0; low, sum n.
    # bisection on lambda range [v.min()-1, v.max()+1]
    lo = v.min() - 1.0
    hi = v.max() + 1.0
    # sum clip is decreasing in lambda.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(v-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # adjust tiny error
    d=target-h.sum()
    if d>0:
       # add to values with room? Feasible set; if error tiny, distribute uniformly? But might violate box. Use water correction:
       if d>1e-12: ...
    return h
```

Let's detail continuous L-BFGS top-level functions:
```
def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, pen, orient):  # orient unused? Need for gradient only
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full')
    r=c*dx
    M=float(r.max())
    if alpha <= 1: return M
    shifted=alpha*(r-M)
    smax=float(shifted.max())
    e=np.exp(shifted-smax)
    sm=np.log(e.sum())
    return M + (smax+sm)/alpha
```
For alpha high, if e sum=0? smax max 0, at least one 1. good.

Gradient:
```
def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,pen,orient):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    r=c*dx
    M=r.max()
    shifted=alpha*(r-M)
    smax=shifted.max()
    e=np.exp(shifted-smax)
    w=e/e.sum()
    wg=w*dx
    a=1.0-2.0*x
    g=weighted_gradient(wg,a,n,orient)
    target=n/2.0
    g += (2.0*pen*(x.sum()-target))*np.ones(n)
    return g
```
Should we include bounds? L-BFGS-B handles.

So we can implement fast delta exactly as previous but optimized with slices using v and h_rev:
```
def add_single_flip(delta, p, v, h_rev, n, sign):
   # sign +1 for +e_p, -1 for -e_p
   delta[n-1-p : n-1-p+n] += sign * v
   delta[p : p+n] -= sign * h_rev
```
For swap i zero +, j one - : sign i=+1, j=-1:
`delta[st_i:st_i+n] += v; delta[i:i+n] -= h_rev`
`delta[st_j:st_j+n] -= v; delta[j:j+n] += h_rev`
Then interactions same. This avoids `a`.

From col_p: for variable p, c_k derivative = (when q=n-1+k):
- contribution if q = n-1 + (r-p) for some r: w_q * v[r] = w_q*(1-h[r])
- contribution if q = n-1 + (p-r) for some r: - w_q*h[r]
For objective φ=∑ w_q c_q, gradient_p = ∑_{q} w_q [ v[p+(q-(n-1))] if valid - h[p-(q-(n-1))] if valid ]. Let k = q-(n-1).

This is similar to previous weighted_gradient but using v and h two parts. We can implement `weighted_gradient(w, h, n)` without requiring orientation:
```
g=zeros(n)
for q,wq:
  if wq==0 continue
  k=q-(n-1)
  if k>=0:
     g[:n-k] += wq * v[k:]
     g[k:] -= wq * h[:n-k]
  else:
     g[-k:] += wq * v[:n+k]
     g[:n+k] -= wq * h[-k:]
```
This should match the numpy convention. We can calibrate this function numerically. Good, no orientation needed.

Simulated annealing implementation:
```
def binary_anneal(h,n,deadline,seed,T0=3.0,cool=0.99997,use_multi=False):
  c,a? v,h_rev
  M=c.max(); best...
  ones_arr = np.where(h==1)[0]; zeros_arr = ...
  rng = RandomState(seed)
  it=0
  no_improve=0
  while time<deadline and it<maxit:
    if rng.random()<0.2 and n large? p=2 else p=1
    choose flips
    compute delta/nc
    d = nc - M  # count difference
    accept if d <=0 or rng.exp Distribution < exp(-d/T)
    if accept: apply multi or swap; update lists (for p=1 update arr; p=2 more complex)
    if nc < best_c_count: best...
    T *= cool; if T<1e-3: T=1e-3 or reheat when no_improve > threshold
```
Maintaining lists for p=2: could just recompute zeros/ones arrays after each accept? O(n) fine if accepts not too many; but for 1M iterations, cannot recompute every random reject. For p=1, update arrays in-place to keep O(1): zeros_arr[ai]=j, ones_arr[bi]=i. For p=2, maybe avoid or recompute occasionally. We can restrict annealing to p=1 for simplicity; p=2 every 1000th with recomputation of lists after accept? If p=2 reject, no recompute. If accept, update h, and mark lists invalid, rebuild. Rebuild cost O(n) at accepted 2-swaps less frequent. Good.

For p=1 local p=1. For multi, if accept, `dirty=True; rebuild arrays`. For p=1 in-place.

Delta for multi p=2 uses current v/h_rev. We'll implement specialized for p=2:

Implement `swap_delta` top-level:
```
def swap_delta(h, v, h_rev, i, j, n, orient, delta):
   delta.fill(0.0)
   if orient == 0: # A
      st = n-1-i; delta[st:st+n] += v; delta[i:i+n] -= h_rev
      st = n-1-j; delta[st:st+n] -= v; delta[j:j+n] += h_rev
   else: # B
      # B col: delta[i:i+n] += v_rev; delta[n-1-i:n-1-i+n] -= h
      # Need v_rev. We can maintain v_rev. But maybe calibrate B? We can include v_rev.
      delta[i:i+n] += v_rev; delta[n-1-i:n-1-i+n] -= h
      delta[j:j+n] -= v_rev; delta[n-1-j:n-1-j+n] += h
   delta[n-1]-=2; delta[n-1+i-j]+=1; delta[n-1+j-i]+=1
   return delta
```
For B, maintain v_rev = v[::-1].copy(), update with h changes. For A, h_rev. To simplify, maintain both `h_rev` and `v_rev`. After swaps update all: h_rev index n-1-p, v_rev same.

But if orientation A likely, B unused. Maintain both anyway cheap: update 4 arrays.

For B, are signs correct for swap Δ=+e_i -e_j? col_i +, col_j -. B col: +: delta[i:i+n]+=v_rev; delta[st:st+n]-=h. -: subtract that: delta[j:j+n]-=v_rev; delta[stj:stj+n]+=h. Yes.

Calibrate B with v_rev/h. Good.

Generate supports:
```
def build_support_masks(n, orient):
    masks=[]
    for p in range(n):
       st=min(p,n-1-p); en=max(p+n-1,2*n-2-p)
       length=en-st+1
       mask=((1<<length)-1) << st
       masks.append(mask)
    return masks
```
L up to 1000; Python int shift fine. n=1000 length 1999, 1<<1999 big but okay. For n=400 L=799.

```
def bool_mask_to_int(booleans):
   m=0
   # maybe use enumerate; for speed if len<=2000 okay
   for idx,val in enumerate(booleans):
      if val: m |= (1<<idx)
   return m
```

Now continuous `project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(v,n,target):
    # if v already close? still project
    lo = float(v.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(v.max()) + 1.0
    # We need sum clip(v-lambda) decreasing in lambda.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(v-mid,0,1))
       if s > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    h=np.clip(v-lam,0,1)
    d=target-h.sum()
    # residual tiny; distribute to values not at bound
    if abs(d)>1e-12:
       interior=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
       cnt=interior.sum()
       if cnt>0:
          h[interior] += d/cnt
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          h += d/n
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
60 iterations uses `np.clip` each, n small. For n=800 maybe okay. Could reduce to 40. Use vectorized.

Pseudo:
```
def binary_greedy(h,n,deadline,orient,max_sweeps=200):
   dx=2/n
   h=h.copy(); c=correlate(h,1-h).astype(float); M=float(c.max())
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx
   v=1-h; h_rev=h[::-1].copy(); v_rev=v[::-1].copy()
   supports=build_support_masks(n)
   zeros=np.where(h==0)[0].tolist(); ones=np.where(h==1)[0].tolist()
   delta=np.zeros(2*n-1); temp=np.empty(2*n-1)
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      M=float(np.max(c)) # integer maybe
      M_int=int(rint(M))
      peaks = c >= M_int - 1e-9? for floats. Use `c >= M - 1e-8`
      pm=...
      improved=False
      # randomize order? Could shuffle zeros/ones each sweep to avoid deterministic bias. But if we shuffle lists, need maintain after swaps. For first improvement, order can affect. Use rng? Could be okay.
      for i in zeros:
         if time>deadline: break
         m_i=supports[i]
         for j in ones:
            if ((m_i | supports[j]) & pm) != pm: continue
            swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,i,j,n,orient,delta)
            np.add(c,delta,out=temp)
            nc float(temp.max())
            if nc < M - 1e-7:
                # apply
                c += delta
                h[i]=1; h[j]=0; v[i]=0; v[j]=1
                ri=n-1-i; rj=n-1-j
                h_rev[ri]=1; v_rev[ri]=0? Wait v=1-h; at i zero->1: v[i]=0; at j one->0: v[j]=1. v_rev at ri (position i reversed) becomes 0; at rj becomes 1. h_rev[ri]=1; h_rev[rj]=0.
                ri = n-1-i; rj = n-1-j
                h_rev[ri]=1.0; h_rev[rj]=0.0
                v[? v array handled]
                v_rev[ri]=0.0; v_rev[rj]=1.0
                ones.remove(j); zeros.remove(i); ones.append(i); zeros.append(j)
                improved=True
                break
         if improved: break
      if not improved: break
      # update best if c max lower
      new_c=c.max()*dx

   return best_h,best_c
```

`smooth_obj/grad` use `orient` argument but formula B if orient=1. Let's implement `weighted_gradient(w,h,n,orient)` with A/B as derived.
```
def weighted_gradient(w,h,n,orient):
    L=2*n-1; g=np.zeros(n); v=1.0-h; m=n-1
    if orient==0:
      for q,wq in enumerate(w):
        if wq==0: continue
        k=q-m
        if k>=0:
           nn=n-k
           if nn>0:
             g[:nn] += wq * v[k:]
             g[k:] -= wq * h[:nn]
        else:
           nn=n+k
           if nn>0:
             g[-k:] += wq * v[:nn]
             g[:nn] -= wq * h[-k:]
    else:
      for q,wq...
        if k>=0:
          nn=n-k
          g[k:] += wq * v[:nn]
          g[:nn] -= wq * h[k:]
        else:
          nn=n+k
          g[:nn] += wq * v[-k:]
          g[-k:] -= wq * h[:nn]
    return g
```
Need verify A formula again with C_k=sum h_p v_{p+k}. For k>=0: p from 0 to n-1-k for h_p v_{p+k}: direct h_p derivative v_{p+k}; g[p]+=w*v[p+k] => g[:n-k]+=v[k:]. v_{p+k} also when flipped? term -h_{p+k} for p? Variation v at q: -h_p? Wait c_k=sum h_p(1-h_{p+k}). Derivative wrt h_i: from h_i in first factor: (1-h_{i+k}) if i+k valid. from second factor of term h_{i-k}(1-h_i): -h_{i-k}. For k>=0: if i<=n-1-k, first term v[i+k]; if i>=k, second -h[i-k]. So g[:n-k]+=v[k:], g[k:]-=h[:n-k]. Yes A.
For B: c_k=sum h_{p+k}(1-h_p). Derivative wrt h_i: from first factor h_i? p+k=i => if i>=k, direct (1-h_{i-k})=v[i-k]; from second factor -h_{i+k}? Actually term h_i? v_i in terms with i=p: -h_{p+k}= -h[i+k]. So for k>=0: if i>=k: g[i]+=v[i-k] -> g[k:]+=v[:n-k]; if i<n-k: g[i]-=h[i+k] -> g[:n-k]-=h[k:]. Yes B.
Good.

```
def calibrate_gradient_orient(n=8):
  rng=RandomState(123)
  x=0.2+0.6*rng.rand(n)
  w = np.abs(rng.randn(2*n-1)); w = w + 0.05; w/=w.sum()
  eps=1e-6
  grad=np.zeros(n)
  for i in range(n):
     xp=x.copy(); xp[i]+=eps
     xm=x.copy(); xm[i]-=eps
     cp=np.correlate(xp,1-xp,'full'); cm=...
     grad[i]=np.dot(w,cp-cm)/(2*eps)
  gA=weighted_gradient(w,x,n,0); gB=...
  errA=norm(gA-grad); errB=norm(gB-grad)
  return 0 if errA<=errB else 1
```

```
def polish_continuous(h0,n,deadline,seed,orient):
```

Detailed implementations:

`compute_c5(h,n)`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full'))*dx)
```
If n=0? not.

`project_box_sum`: Use bisection. Need `v` may be integer/h. The projection solution: minimize (h-v)^2 s.t. 0≤h≤1,sum=T. The water level λ. Sum decreasing in λ. At λ very low (<minv), sum=n; high, 0. We set lo = min(v)-1, hi=max(v)+1. We want sum target. If sum(mid)>target, need larger λ -> lo=mid; else hi=mid. After, h=clip(v-(lo+hi)/2). Good.
Residual distribution: if d>0 and no interior? just uniform then clip; residual maybe not exact but tiny. For exact integer target, binary h sum exact already; project not called on binary except perhaps after interpolation.

`to_binary(h,n)`:
```
def to_binary(h,n):
    hb=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:n//2]
    hb[idx]=1.0
    return hb
```
If n odd? ensure even.

`default_h(n,seed)`:
```
def default_h(n,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    x=rng.rand(n)
    return project_box_sum(x,n,n/2.0)
```

`swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,v_rev,i,j,n,orient,delta)`:
```
def swap_delta(...):
    delta.fill(0.0)
    n1=n-1
    if orient==0:
        s=n1-i
        delta[s:s+n] += v
        delta[i:i+n] -= h_rev
        s=n1-j
        delta[s:s+n] -= v
        delta[j:j+n] += h_rev
    else:
        delta[i:i+n] += v_rev
        delta[n1-i:n1-i+n] -= h
        delta[j:j+n] -= v_rev
        delta[n1-j:n1-j+n] += h
    delta[n1] -= 2.0
    delta[n1 + i - j] += 1.0
    delta[n1 + j - i] += 1.0
    return delta
```
Need ensure slices with negative? i 0..n-1; n1-i >=0; n1-i+n <=? for i=0: n-1+n=2n-1 length n end L. Good.

`multi_delta` generic:
```
def multi_delta(h,v,h_rev,v_rev,plus,minus,n,orient,delta):
    delta.fill(0)
    n1=n-1
    def? no closures. We'll loop.
    for p in plus: add sign + using code inline via helper `add_single`? Need top-level helper to avoid duplication.
```
Define `add_single(delta,p,sign,h,v,h_rev,v_rev,n,orient)`. That is top-level. Use for multi calibration. Overhead okay.
```
def add_single(delta,p,sign,h,v,h_rev,v_rev,n,orient):
   n1=n-1
   if orient==0:
      s=n1-p
      if sign>0:
         delta[s:s+n] += v
         delta[p:p+n] -= h_rev
      else:
         delta[s:s+n] -= v
         delta[p:p+n] += h_rev
   else:
      s=n1-p
      if sign>0:
         delta[p:p+n] += v_rev
         delta[s:s+n] -= h
      else:
         delta[p:p+n] -= v_rev
         delta[s:s+n] += h
```
Then `multi_delta`:
```
def multi_delta(h,v,h_rev,v_rev,plus,minus,n,orient,delta):
   delta.fill(0)
   for p in plus: add_single(...,1)
   for p in minus: add_single(...,-1)
   n1=n-1
   cnt=len(plus)+len(minus)
   delta[n1] -= float(cnt)
   pos=list(plus)+list(minus); coeff=[1.0]*len(plus)+[-1.0]*len(minus)
   m=len(pos)
   for a in range(m):
      pa=pos[a]; ca=coeff[a]
      for b in range(a+1,m):
         off=pa-pos[b]
         val=-ca*coeff[b]
         if val:
            delta[n1+off] += val
            delta[n1-off] += val
   return delta
```
For p=1 single, using multi with plus [i], minus [j] would do same but more overhead. Use specialized for speed.

`build_support_masks(n)` doesn't need orient:
```
def build_support_masks(n):
    n1=n-1; masks=[]
    for p in range(n):
       st=p if p < n1-p else n1-p
       ee=p+n-1
       alt=2*n1-p
       if alt>ee: ee=alt
       length=ee-st+1
       masks.append(((1<<length)-1)<<st)
    return masks
```
Need L=2n-1, alt max 2n-2-p <=2n-2, okay.

`bools_to_mask(booleans)`:
```
def bools_to_mask(booleans):
    m=0
    for idx in np.flatnonzero(booleans):
       m |= 1 << int(idx)
    return m
```
Using np.flatnonzero faster.

`binary_greedy` implementation details:
```
def binary_greedy(h,n,deadline,orient,max_sweeps=200):
    dx=2.0/n; n1=n-1; L=2*n-1
    h=np.array(h,float).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
    M=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx
    v=1.0-h; h_rev=h[::-1].copy(); v_rev=v[::-1].copy()
    zeros=list(np.where(h==0)[0].astype(int)); ones=list(...1)
    supports=build_support_masks(n)
    delta=np.zeros(L); temp=np.empty(L)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       M=int(round(float(c.max())))
       peaks_mask=bools_to_mask(c >= M - 1e-8)
       improved=False
       timer_counter=0
       for i in zeros:
          if improved or time.time()>deadline: break
          mi=supports[i]
          for j in ones:
             timer_counter +=1
             if (timer_counter & 1023)==0 and time.time()>deadline: improved? break? Use flag.
             if ((mi | supports[j]) & peaks_mask) != peaks_mask: continue
             swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,v_rev,i,j,n,orient,delta)
             np.add(c,delta,out=temp)
             nc=float(temp.max())
             if nc < M - 1e-7:
                c += delta
                h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0
                v[i]=0.0; v[j]=1.0
                ri=n1-i; rj=n1-j
                h_rev[ri]=1.0; h_rev[rj]=0.0
                v_rev[ri]=0.0; v_rev[rj]=1.0
                # update lists
                # list remove O(n)
                ones.remove(j); zeros.remove(i); ones.append(i); zeros.append(j)
                improved=True
                break
          if time.time()>deadline and not improved: break
       if not improved: break
       val=float(c.max())*dx
       if val < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=val; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
Inside inner, if time exceeded we might break but `improved` false; outer breaks. Use `timed_out` flag. Simpler: check `if time.time()>deadline: break` at top of inner every 512 candidates; if break, set `if not improved: break` outer by checking time after inner.

List `remove` with value j might remove first occurrence; values unique. Good.

`M` as int count; if c max not exactly integer due floats, when apply, max c maybe M-1 but float. Use M int. If nc < M - 1e-7. If M count might be 77, nc 77.000? no improvement. Good.

`binary_anneal` implementation details:
```
def binary_anneal(h,n,deadline,seed,orient,use_multi_prob=0.12):
   dx=2/n; n1=n-1; L=2*n-1
   h=np.array(h,float).copy(); c=...
   M=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx; best_count=M
   v=1-h; h_rev=...; v_rev=...
   zeros=np.where(h==0)[0].astype(int); ones=np.where(h==1)[0].astype(int)

      it +=1
```

Let's write `calibrate_delta_orientation`:
```
def calibrate_delta_orientation():
    n=8; rng=np.random.RandomState(9)
    best_err=1e100; best_o=0; L=2*n-1
    for _ in range(4):
        h=np.zeros(n); idx=rng.choice(n,size=n//2,replace=False); h[idx]=1
        v=1-h; h_rev=h[::-1].copy(); v_rev=v[::-1].copy()
        zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
        i=int(rng.choice(zeros)); j=int(rng.choice(ones))
        c0=np.correlate(h,v,'full')
        h2=h.copy(); h2[i]=1; h2[j]=0; c1=np.correlate(h2,1-h2,'full')
        desired=c1-c0
        for o in (0,1):
            delta=np.zeros(L); swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,v_rev,i,j,n,o,delta)
            err=float(np.linalg.norm(delta-desired))
            if err<best_err: best_err=err; best_o=o
    return best_o
```
`rng.choice(n,size=n//2)` works n//2=4; returns ndarray ints. Good.

`calibrate_gradient_orientation` use n=8. Ensure x not exactly at bounds. `w` all positive. Numerical gradient: cP/cM; w dot difference. Note if objective linear in c (no dx), gradient formula without dx. We pass w directly. Good.

Let's write `weighted_gradient` with local variables and orient branch outside loop to avoid branch per q:
```
def weighted_gradient(w,h,n,orient):
    g=np.zeros(n); v=1.0-h; n1=n-1
    if orient == 0:
        for q,wq in enumerate(w):
           if wq == 0.0: continue
           k=q-n1
           if k>=0:
              a=n-k
              if a>0:
                 g[:a] += wq * v[k:]
                 g[k:] -= wq * h[:a]
           else:
              a=n+k
              if a>0:
                 g[-k:] += wq * v[:a]
                 g[:a] -= wq * h[-k:]
    else: ...
    return g
```
Note `g[-k:]` when k negative: -k positive. `g[:a]`. Good.

```
best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

```
def project_feasible(h):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=len(h)/2
  for _ in range(50):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
       room=1-h; s=room.sum(); if s<1e-14: break; h += diff/s*room
    else:
       s=h.sum(); if s<1e-14: break; h += diff/s*h
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This is ~15 lines, not Euclidean projection but feasible. It may change h less? It uses multiplicative for reduce? For diff<0, `h += diff/s*h` scales h by (1+diff/s) = target/s, reducing proportionally. For diff>0, adds to room proportionally. It may be less optimal but okay. `project_box_sum` more robust but longer. Use previous project_feasible to save lines. It ensures sum within 1e-13. Could final sum exact enough. Use `project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h):
    n=len(h); target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        d=target-h.sum()
        if abs(d)<1e-13: break
        if d>0:
            r=1.0-h; s=r.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += d/s*r
        else:
            s=h.sum()
            if s<1e-14: break
            h += d/s*h
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
This `h += d/s*h` when d negative: h*(1+d/s)=h*target/s. Good.

Now, `swap_delta` A:
```
def swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,i,j,n,delta):
    delta.fill(0.0); n1=n-1
    s=n1-i
    delta[s:s+n] += v
    delta[i:i+n] -= h_rev
    s=n1-j
    delta[s:s+n] -= v
    delta[j:j+n] += h_rev
    delta[n1] -= 2.0
    delta[n1+i-j] += 1.0
    delta[n1+j-i] += 1.0
```
Need `h_rev = h[::-1].copy()`. Good.

In binary_greedy update v and h_rev. We don't maintain v_rev. Good.

`bools_to_mask`:
```
def mask_from_peak(booleans):
    m=0
    for q in np.flatnonzero(booleans):
        m |= 1 << int(q)
    return m
```

`binary_greedy` compact:
```
def binary_greedy(h,n,deadline,max_sweeps=200):
    dx=2.0/n; n1=n-1; L=2*n-1
    h=h.copy(); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full').astype(float)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.max()*dx
    v=1-h; h_rev=h[::-1].copy()
    zeros=list(np.where(h==0)[0]); ones=list(np.where(h==1)[0])
    supp=build_support_masks(n)
    delta=np.zeros(L); temp=np.empty(L)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        if time.time()>deadline: break
        M=int(round(c.max()))
        pm=mask_from_peak(c >= M-1e-8)
        improved=False; it=0
        for i in zeros:
            if improved or time.time()>deadline: break
            si=supp[i]
            for j in ones:
                it += 1
                if (it & 4095)==0 and time.time()>deadline: break
                if ((si | supp[j]) & pm) != pm: continue
                swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,i,j,n,delta)
                np.add(c,delta,out=temp)
                if temp.max() < M - 1e-7:
                    c += delta
                    h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0
                    v[i]=0.0; v[j]=1.0
                    ri=n1-i; rj=n1-j
                    h_rev[ri]=1.0; h_rev[rj]=0.0
                    ones.remove(j); zeros.remove(i); ones.append(i); zeros.append(j)
                    improved=True
                    break
            if time.time()>deadline: break
        if not improved: break
        val=c.max()*dx
        if val < best_c:
            best_c=val; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
`zeros` and `ones` lists of numpy ints; list remove okay. In inner if time break, `improved` false; outer breaks.

`binary_anneal` compact single:
```
def binary_anneal(h,n,deadline,seed):
    dx=2.0/n; n1=n-1; L=2*n-1
    h=h.copy(); c=...
    M=float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*dx; bestM=M
    v=1-h; h_rev=h[::-1].copy()
    z=np.where(h==0)[0].astype(int); o=np.where(h==1)[0].astype(int)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    T=0.7; cooling=0.99999; delay=0; delta=np.zeros(L); temp=np.empty(L)
    while time.time()<deadline:
        if delay > 40000 and T < 0.6:
            T=0.8; delay=0
        ai=int(rng.randint(len(z))); bi=int(rng.randint(len(o)))
        i=int(z[ai]); j=int(o[bi])
        swap_delta(h,v,h_rev,i,j,n,delta)
        np.add(c,delta,out=temp); nc=float(temp.max())
        d=nc-M
        if d <= 1e-9:
            accept=True
        elif T>1e-12:
            a=-d/T
            accept = (a > -700) and (rng.random() < math.exp(a))
        else:
            accept=False
        if accept:
            c += delta
            h[i]=1.0; h[j]=0.0
            v[i]=0.0; v[j]=1.0
            ri=n1-i; rj=n1-j
            h_rev[ri]=1.0; h_rev[rj]=0.0
            M=nc
            z[ai]=j; o[bi]=i
            if M < bestM - 1e-9:
                bestM=M; best_c=M*dx; best_h=h.copy(); delay=0
            else:
                delay += 1
        else:
            delay += 1
        T = max(0.03, T*cooling)
    return best_h,best_c
```
If T reheat to 0.8, then cooling multiplies; okay. If delay large but T high, not reheat. Fine.

`weighted_gradient` A compact:
```
def grad_weights(w,h,n):
    g=np.zeros(n); v=1-h; n1=n-1
    for q,wq in enumerate(w):
        if wq == 0.0: continue
        k=q-n1
        if k>=0:
            a=n-k; a += 0? if a>0:
                g[:a] += wq*v[k:]
                g[k:] -= wq*h[:a]
        else:
            a=n+k
            if a>0:
                g[-k:] += wq*v[:a]
                g[:a] -= wq*h[-k:]
    return g
```
Check for k>=0, a = n-k. If k=0, g[:n] += v[0:]; g[0:] -= h[0:]. Good. For k negative, a positive. `g[-k:]` length n+k? If k=-1, `g[1:]` length n-1; `v[:a]` length a=n-1; good. `h[-k:]` length a. Good.

`smooth_obj`:
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,pen):
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*(2.0/n)
    m=c.max()
    if pen:
        s=x.sum()-0.5*n
        m += pen*s*s
    sh=alpha*(c-m) # Wait if m includes penalty, objective non-linear? Penalty should be separate; logsum only on c, not penalty.
```
Oops objective = smooth(max c) + penalty. Cannot add penalty to m before logsum. Do:
```
    cc=np.correlate...
    dx=2/n; r=cc*dx; base=r.max(); sh=alpha*(r-base); s=...; smooth=base+(smax+logsum)/alpha
    if pen:
       sm=x.sum()-0.5*n; smooth += pen*sm*sm
```
Good.

`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,pen):
    dx=2/n; r=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*dx
    base=r.max(); sh=alpha*(r-base); smax=sh.max(); e=np.exp(sh-smax); w=e/e.sum()
    g=grad_weights(w*dx,x,n)
    if pen:
       s=x.sum()-0.5*n; g += 2*pen*s*np.ones(n)
    return g
```
Good.

`polish_continuous` compact:
```
def polish_continuous(h,n,deadline,seed):
    h=project_feasible(h.copy())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    alphas=[20,100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000]
    pens=[1e-4,1e-4,1e-3,1e-3,1e-2,0.1,1.0]
    maxit=max(60,int(60000/n))
    x=h.copy()
    for idx,a in enumerate(alphas):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       p=pens[idx]
       try:
          res=minimize(smooth_obj,x,args=(n,a,p),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=smooth_grad,
                       options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-5,'disp':False})
          xx=res.x
       except Exception:
          xx=x
       xx=project_feasible(xx)
       cc=compute_c5(xx,n)
       if cc < best_c + 1e-10:
          x=xx.copy()
          if cc < best_c:
             best_c=cc; best_h=xx.copy()
       else:
          x=best_h.copy()
    # restarts
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
       rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
       for trial in range(3):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          xx=project_feasible(best_h + 0.01*rng.normal(size=n))
          for a,p in [(20000,0.1),(100000,1.0)]:
             if time.time()>deadline: break
             try: res=minimize(... maxit=max(50,maxit//2)) ...
             xx=project_feasible(...)
             cc=...
             if cc < best_c: best... x=xx
             else: x=best_h
    return best_h,best_c
```
Need `maxit` variable for restart. Good.

Maybe `smooth_obj` and `smooth_grad` compute `x.sum()` etc. Good.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); total=max(30,budget_s-10); deadline=t0+total
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,float).copy(); n0=len(h0)
       if n0<2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)): raise ValueError
    except Exception:
       n0=400; h0=np.random.RandomState(seed).rand(n0)
    if n0%2: n0-=1; h0=h0[:n0]
    h0=project_feasible(h0)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h0,n0); best_n=n0
    b0=to_binary(h0,n0); bc=compute_c5(b0,n0)
    if bc<best_c: best_c=bc; best_h=b0.copy()
    p1=t0+0.07*total
    hg,cg=binary_greedy(b0,n0,p1)
    if cg<best_c: best...
    p2=t0+0.33*total
    ha,ca=binary_anneal(hg,n0,p2,seed+1)
    if ca<best_c: update
    p3=t0+0.70*total
    hq,cq=polish_continuous(best_h,best_n,p3,seed+2)
    if cq<best_c: update
    if time.time() < p3? no
    p4=t0+0.88*total
    nh=300 if best_n !=300 else 400? If best_n=300, try 400.
    for nh in ([300] if best_n!=300 else [400]):
       if time.time()>p4: break
       xo=np.linspace(0,2,best_n); xn=np.linspace(0,2,nh)
       hh=project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,best_h))
       bb=to_binary(hh,nh)
       bb,c_b=binary_greedy(bb,nh,min(p4-20,time.time()+60))
       if c_b<best_c: update
       if time.time() < p4-10:
          bb2,c_a=binary_anneal(bb,nh,p4-5,seed+1000)
          if c_a<best_c: update
       if time.time() < p4-2:
          hq,c_q=polish_continuous(bb,nh,p4,seed+2000)
          if c_q<best_c: update
    if time.time() < deadline-15:
       hq,cq=polish_continuous(best_h,best_n,deadline-2,seed+7)
       if cq<best_c: update
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h,best_c,best_n
```

But random could be far; anneal maybe. Could generate a structured default: e.g. `h0 = (np.linspace(0,1,n) < 0.5)` interval, C=0.5 worse. Random maybe similar. Since parent available, no worry.

Let's produce code with semicolons? Python allows multiple statements per line but maybe less readable; allowed. We can keep under 300 by using compact one-liners. Need about 12k chars. We can be concise.

Let's maybe use `project_feasible` with 60 loops. okay.

N=400, 40k evals per scan ~ maybe 0.5-1s. Greedy needs <50 scans => 50s. Fine. We can remove support prefilter to save ~14 lines and reduce complexity. But if many swaps, earlier first improvement? One more thought: `np.correlate` may be slower for double length 400? okay.

`binary_anneal` uses `rng.randint(len(z))`; if len zero? n half ones, both nonzero. Good.

`binary_greedy` list remove of numpy int: Python list contains numpy ints; `remove(j)` compares equal. okay. But after `zeros.remove(i)`, if duplicate? no. If `i` not in list because we updated? We only apply once per sweep and break; lists consistent. In next sweep, lists include updated. Good.

In `binary_greedy`, if `it & 4095` time break, but if time break and no improvement, outer breaks due time check at top next? It breaks inner, then `if time.time()>deadline: break` outer. Good.

If objective/gradient has NaN due overflow? logsum stable. Penalty maybe large but x bounded. If `math.exp` not in smooth, okay.

Let's ensure final `best_c` returned after final projection. If final projection makes C worse than an alt, we don't have fallback. But since best_h was feasible/projected, okay. For continuous `polish_continuous` returns projected best_h and best_c computed after. Final project_feasible can slightly change due iterative projection not idempotent? It is approximately idempotent; if sum residual <1e-13, clip maybe same. Could change values tiny and C maybe 1e-15. fine.

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h):
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * len(h)
    for _ in range(80):
        d = target - h.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - h
            s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (d / s) * r
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            h += (d / s) * h
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

def to_binary(h, n):
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    hb[np.argsort(h)[::-1][: n // 2]] = 1.0
    return hb

def swap_delta(h, v, h_rev, i, j, n, delta):
    delta.fill(0.0)
    n1 = n - 1
    s = n1 - i
    delta[s:s + n] += v
    delta[i:i + n] -= h_rev
    s = n1 - j
    delta[s:s + n] -= v
    delta[j:j + n] += h_rev
    delta[n1] -= 2.0
    delta[n1 + i - j] += 1.0
    delta[n1 + j - i] += 1.0

def build_support_masks(n):
    n1 = n - 1
    out = []
    for p in range(n):
        st = p if p < n1 - p else n1 - p
        ee = max(p + n - 1, 2 * n - 1 - p)
        out.append(((1 << (ee - st + 1)) - 1) << st)
    return out

def mask_from_peak(booleans):
    m = 0
    for q in np.flatnonzero(booleans):
        m |= 1 << int(q)
    return m

def binary_greedy(h, n, deadline):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    n1 = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(c.max()) * dx
    v = 1.0 - h
    h_rev = h[::-1].copy()
    zeros = list(np.where(h == 0)[0].astype(int))
    ones = list(np.where(h == 1)[0].astype(int))
    supp = build_support_masks(n)
    delta = np.zeros(L)
    temp = np.empty(L)
    for sweep in range(300):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        M = int(round(float(c.max())))
        pm = mask_from_peak(c >= M - 1e-8)
        improved = False
        it = 0
        for i in zeros:
            if improved or time.time() > deadline:
                break
            si = supp[i]
            for j in ones:
                it += 1
                if (it & 4095) == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                if ((si | supp[j]) & pm) != pm:
                    continue
                swap_delta(h, v, h_rev, i, j, n, delta)
                np.add(c, delta, out=temp)
                if float(temp.max()) < M - 1e-7:
                    c += delta
                    h[i] = 1.0
                    h[j] = 0.0
                    v[i] = 0.0
                    v[j] = 1.0
                    ri = n1 - i
                    rj = n1 - j
                    h_rev[ri] = 1.0
                    h_rev[rj] = 0.0
                    ones.remove(j)
                    zeros.remove(i)
                    ones.append(i)
                    zeros.append(j)
                    improved = True
                    break
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
        if not improved:
            break
        val = float(c.max()) * dx
        if val < best_c - 1e-14:
            best_c = val
            best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def binary_anneal(h, n, deadline, seed):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    n1 = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = M * dx
    bestM = M
    v = 1.0 - h
    h_rev = h[::-1].copy()
    z = np.where(h == 0)[0].astype(int)
    o = np.where(h == 1)[0].astype(int)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    T = 0.7
    delay = 0
    delta = np.zeros(L)
    temp = np.empty(L)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if delay > 40000 and T < 0.6:
            T = 0.8
            delay = 0
        ai = int(rng.randint(len(z)))
        bi = int(rng.randint(len(o)))
        i = int(z[ai])
        j = int(o[bi])
        swap_delta(h, v, h_rev, i, j, n, delta)
        np.add(c, delta, out=temp)
        nc = float(temp.max())
        d = nc - M
        if d <= 1e-9:
            accept = True
        elif T > 1e-12:
            a = -d / T
            accept = (a > -700.0) and (rng.random() < math.exp(a))
        else:
            accept = False
        if accept:
            c += delta
            h[i] = 1.0
            h[j] = 0.0
            v[i] = 0.0
            v[j] = 1.0
            ri = n1 - i
            rj = n1 - j
            h_rev[ri] = 1.0
            h_rev[rj] = 0.0
            M = nc
            z[ai] = j
            o[bi] = i
            if M < bestM - 1e-9:
                bestM = M
                best_c = M * dx
                best_h = h.copy()
                delay = 0
            else:
                delay += 1
        else:
            delay += 1
        T = max(0.03, T * 0.99999)
    return best_h, best_c

def grad_weights(w, h, n):
    g = np.zeros(n)
    v = 1.0 - h
    n1 = n - 1
    for q, wq in enumerate(w):
        if wq == 0.0:
            continue
        k = q - n1
        if k >= 0:
            a = n - k
            if a > 0:
                g[:a] += wq * v[k:]
                g[k:] -= wq * h[:a]
        else:
            a = n + k
            if a > 0:
                g[-k:] += wq * v[:a]
                g[:a] -= wq * h[-k:]
    return g

def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, pen):
    r = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(r.max())
    sh = alpha * (r - m)
    sm = float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh - sm)
    out = m + (sm + float(np.log(e.sum()))) / alpha
    if pen:
        s = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
        out += pen * s * s
    return float(out)

def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, pen):
    r = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(r.max())
    sh = alpha * (r - m)
    sm = float(sh.max())
    e = np.exp(sh - sm)
    w = e / float(e.sum())
    g = grad_weights(w * (2.0 / n), x, n)
    if pen:
        s = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
        g += (2.0 * pen * s) * np.ones(n)
    return g

def polish_continuous(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    h = project_feasible(h0)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    alphas = [20, 100, 500, 2000, 10000, 50000, 200000]
    pens = [1e-4, 1e-4, 1e-3, 1e-3, 1e-2, 0.1, 1.0]
    maxit = max(60, int(55000 / n))
    x = h.copy()
    for idx, a in enumerate(alphas):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        p = pens[idx]
        try:
            res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(n, a, p), method="L-BFGS-B",
                           bounds=bounds, jac=smooth_grad,
                           options={"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-5, "disp": False})
            xx = res.x
        except Exception:
            xx = x
        xx = project_feasible(xx)
        cc = compute_c5(xx, n)
        if cc <= best_c + 1e-10:
            x = xx.copy()
            if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = xx.copy()
        else:
            x = best_h.copy()
    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        small_it = max(40, maxit // 2)
        for trial in range(3):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            x = project_feasible(best_h + 0.01 * rng.normal(size=n))
            for a, p in [(20000, 0.1), (100000, 1.0)]:
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                try:
                    res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(n, a, p), method="L-BFGS-B",
                                   bounds=bounds, jac=smooth_grad,
                                   options={"maxiter": small_it, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-5, "disp": False})
                    xx = res.x
                except Exception:
                    xx = x
                xx = project_feasible(xx)
                cc = compute_c5(xx, n)
                if cc < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = cc
                    best_h = xx.copy()
                    x = xx.copy()
                else:
                    x = best_h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(30, int(budget_s) - 12)
    deadline = t0 + total
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = len(h0)
        if n0 < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h0)):
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.random.RandomState(seed).rand(n0)
    if n0 % 2:
        n0 -= 1
        h0 = h0[:n0]
    h0 = project_feasible(h0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h0, best_h.shape[0])
    best_n = best_h.shape[0]
    b0 = to_binary(h0, best_n)
    bc = compute_c5(b0, best_n)
    if bc < best_c:
        best_c = bc
        best_h = b0.copy()

    p1 = t0 + 0.07 * total
    hg, cg = binary_greedy(b0, best_n, p1)
    if cg < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_c = cg
        best_h = hg.copy()

    p2 = t0 + 0.33 * total
    ha, ca = binary_anneal(hg, best_n, p2, seed + 1)
    if ca < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_c = ca
        best_h = ha.copy()

    p3 = t0 + 0.70 * total
    hq, cq = polish_continuous(best_h, best_n, p3, seed + 2)
    if cq < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_c = cq
        best_h = hq.copy()
        best_n = int(best_h.shape[0])

    p4 = t0 + 0.88 * total
    alt_ns = [300] if best_n != 300 else [400]
    for nh in alt_ns:
        if time.time() > p4:
            break
        if nh < 50 or nh > 800 or nh % 2:
            continue
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, best_n)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nh)
        h_nh = project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, best_h))
        b_nh = to_binary(h_nh, nh)
        dl = max(time.time() + 8, min(p4 - 20, time.time() + 60))
        b_nh, c_b = binary_greedy(b_nh, nh, dl)
        if c_b < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c_b
            best_h = b_nh.copy()
            best_n = nh
        if time.time() < p4 - 10:
            b_nh2, c_a = binary_anneal(b_nh, nh, p4 - 5, seed + 1000)
            if c_a < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c_a
                best_h = b_nh2.copy()
                best_n = nh
        if time.time() < p4 - 3:
            h_nh2, c_q = polish_continuous(b_nh, nh, p4, seed + 2000)
            if c_q < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c_q
                best_h = h_nh2.copy()
                best_n = int(h_nh2.shape[0])

    if time.time() < deadline - 12:
        h_fin, c_fin = polish_continuous(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2, seed + 7)
        if c_fin < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c_fin
            best_h = h_fin.copy()
            best_n = int(best_h.shape[0])

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
